## Diplomacy digest

**At war:**

- imperial ↔ ork — public ExistentialEnemy, secret ExistentialEnemy, treaty Vendetta (tension 100; Open frontier war; standing extermination order.)

**Hostile:**

- leagues_of_votann ↔ ork — public Hostile, secret Hostile, treaty Vendetta (tension 41; Orks raid kin convoys; the Kindreds answer with disproportionate force.)
